# Vlad Petrichev's restoration claimant (VAD) during the civil war.

focus_tree = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_focus

	# Each phase has a separate coordinate band and one absolute anchor; other
	# focuses use a prerequisite as relative_position_id. Keep cells unique.
	# Clausewitz routes links through their vertical midpoint: even row gaps
	# need a clear horizontal corridor.
	# Doctrine branches attach to variant settlements. Late-war availability
	# belongs in available so the branch stays visible before the showdown.
	country = {
		factor = 0
		modifier = {
			add = 100
			tag = VAD
		}
	}
	default = no
	continuous_focus_position = { x = 100 y = 555555 }

	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
	# VAD: negotiated restoration versus the commandanture system.
	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

	focus = {
		id = VAD_proclaim_joint_charter
		icon = GFX_focus_VAD_proclaim_joint_charter
		search_filters = {
			FOCUS_FILTER_MANPOWER
			FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY
			FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL
		}
		x = 44
		y = 7
		cost = 1
		allow_branch = {
			tag = VAD
			OR = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_collapse
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_regional_consolidation
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_preparation
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_showdown
			}
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_focus_phase_tt
				OR = {
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_collapse
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_regional_consolidation
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_preparation
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_showdown
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_if_invalid = yes
		bypass = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_joint_charter }
		completion_reward = {
			unlock_decision_tooltip = VAD_give_the_staff_priority
			unlock_decision_tooltip = VAD_hear_the_district_petitions
			add_political_power = 15
			add_stability = 0.02
			add_manpower = 250
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_joint_charter
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.10 days = 1 }
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { factor = 2 has_stability < 0.50 }
		}
	}

	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
	# VAD variant depth: whose name the restoration is carried out in.
	#
	# Imperial line (NOT ADISCORD_vorkerland_joint_government_formed).
	# Vlad Petrichev restores a hierarchy. Play pattern: hoard political power
	# and spend it on chancery decrees, buy officer quality and planning rather
	# than bodies, keep conscription low and the staff excellent, and pay for
	# it with a permanently unhappy rear. Diplomacy is recognition-seeking -
	# cheap war goals and better standing with anyone who prefers a legitimist
	# Vorkerland to a revolutionary one.
	#
	# Joint line after the council takes office.
	# The rescued chairman and the district guard govern together. Play
	# pattern: trade command quality and political power for reach - broad
	# conscription, high stability, a surrender limit that makes the state very
	# hard to knock over, and slow, wide, resilient offensives. Diplomacy runs
	# through the worker districts rather than through the crown.
	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
